// ABOUTME: Throwaway: card workflow v2 — v1 plus an unguarded prepare activity ahead of the dispatch.
const { proxyActivities } = require('@temporalio/workflow');
const { pinnedDispatch } = require('./wf-common');
async function card(input) {
  return pinnedDispatch(input, async (os) => {
    const { prepare } = proxyActivities({ taskQueue: `rigger-${os}`, startToCloseTimeout: '30s' });
    await prepare({ card: input.card });
  });
}
module.exports = { card };

// ABOUTME: Throwaway: card workflow v3 — the v2 prepare call guarded by patched().
const { proxyActivities, patched } = require('@temporalio/workflow');
const { pinnedDispatch } = require('./wf-common');
async function card(input) {
  return pinnedDispatch(input, async (os) => {
    if (patched('prepare-before-dispatch')) {
      const { prepare } = proxyActivities({ taskQueue: `rigger-${os}`, startToCloseTimeout: '30s' });
      await prepare({ card: input.card });
    }
  });
}
module.exports = { card };

=== APPENDIX: commands run outside the logging wrapper ===
These are the commands the spike engineer ran on the laptop from its own shell (zsh), not through t.sh, w.sh or p.sh, recovered from the session's own conversation. Each is quoted as it was run, with two substitutions. First, these names stand for the same text throughout:
  W  = /Users/cjwilliams/GitHub/rigger-worktrees/1429-spike-engineer
  S  = W/spikes/1429-spike-engineer   (scripts set it as S=~/GitHub/rigger-worktrees/1429-spike-engineer/spikes/1429-spike-engineer); "S=…;" at the start of a line stands for that assignment
  SP = /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-cjwilliams-GitHub-rigger/17bfb854-c7ed-40ce-af2d-5578b0a83820/scratchpad
Second, a heredoc body is replaced by [body: <file>], and that body is quoted verbatim in the section "HEREDOC AND PATCH BODIES" at the end of this file.
Times are UTC. A time marked ~ is reconstructed from neighbouring logged entries, to within a minute; the others are taken from the command's own output or the logged transcript. Output is quoted where the command failed or changed something; read-only commands whose output fed the report are covered by the evidence files the report cites.

The gaps in this record, which the report also lists:
  G1. The pre-commit and pre-push hooks, the suite and the slot each started many short processes; only their summaries and failures are recorded (suite-runs/ and the lines below).
  G2. Non-shell tool calls are listed by kind, time and target only: the Write and Edit calls that wrote and revised the report, the journal and SP/1429-appendix.txt (their cumulative result is the committed files), Read calls on the report and the acceptance skill, Monitor watches, TaskStop calls and one WebFetch. Their full arguments are not reproduced.
  G3. The intermediate uncommitted states of the report and journal between those edits are not kept.
  G4. A command that extracted this list from the session's on-disk record was refused by Claude Code's permission classifier at about 21:45Z on 2026-11-20, so the list below is recovered from the conversation instead of from that file, and its ~ times are reconstructions.
  G5. Some entries summarise a group of commands instead of quoting each one: the entries at ~01:15 (the first assembly of this file), ~01:18 (the perl and sed corrections to the report's times, after the first), ~01:19, ~01:56, ~01:57, ~02:27, ~16:17 (the report revisions after the #1435 review), ~17:05, ~21:40, ~21:45, ~21:50 and ~23:58. Those commands edited the report, the journal entry, the PR draft and this file, or re-ran the read-only checks named in the entry. They were not transcribed one by one. Their results are the committed files, and the report claims nothing through them.
  G6. Where an entry writes "…" inside a command, or a placeholder in angle brackets, the omitted text is named beside it: a description now on the first line of an evidence file, a commit message in git, or a pid list or command logged in full above.
  G7. One word in the ~01:34 entry is written with brackets, as corp[u]s, because the repository's ruled-out-word check (scripts/ruled-out-word-check.mjs) refuses it in any tracked file; the command as run had the plain word.

=== HEREDOC AND PATCH BODIES ===
Quoted under rigger-ae's ruling of 2026-11-20 for #1429: heredoc and patch bodies in a transcript are evidence, not merged spike code. Nothing runs or imports them here; the runnable files stay in the gitignored spikes directory. Each heredoc is quoted as it was written, at its command and time (a time marked ~ is reconstructed to within a minute), with the sha256 of its body. Each in-place edit is quoted as run, in order, and each edited file ends with the sha256 of its final content. The original bodies of the edited files were checked by replaying every edit below on them in a scratch directory, which reproduced each file's final sha256 exactly. S, SP and W are the abbreviations defined at the start of the appendix.

--- ~00:39  cat > S/t.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/bash
# ABOUTME: Throwaway transcript wrapper: logs UTC time, host label, command and output for spike #1429.
# usage: t.sh <hostlabel> <command string run by bash -c on the laptop>
T=$HOME/GitHub/rigger-worktrees/1429-spike-engineer/spikes/1429-spike-engineer/transcript.log
H="$1"; shift
C="$*"
{ printf '\n=== %s | host=%s | cmd: %s\n' "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)" "$H" "$C"; } >> "$T"
bash -c "$C" 2>&1 | tee -a "$T"
rc=${PIPESTATUS[0]}
printf -- '--- exit=%s at %s\n' "$rc" "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)" >> "$T"
exit $rc
EOF

**Stated intent, #1429 spike engineer** (posted before any install or host-configuration change)

Installs, each into a directory of the spike's own making, none system-wide:

- **WSL2 (`hostpc`, reached by `ssh hostpc wsl.exe -e …`):** the Node 24 LTS linux-x64 tarball from nodejs.org and the Temporal CLI linux-amd64 tarball from temporal.download, unpacked under `~/1429-spike-engineer/`. - **Windows (`hostpc`):** the Temporal CLI windows-amd64 zip, unpacked under `C:\Users\user\1429-spike-engineer\`. Then the same `npm install` there with the cache inside it. Node 24 is already installed.

Host-configuration changes: **none intended.** The server runs in WSL2 on 7233/8233 with `--ip 0.0.0.0`. Windows reaches it through WSL2's localhost forwarding. The laptop reaches it through an SSH local forward (`ssh -L 7233:localhost:7233 hostpc`). There is no firewall rule and no port proxy. If either route fails, I will post the change here before I make it.

Processes: one laptop SSH session carries the tunnel and keeps WSL awake for the server. Every pid is recorded in the transcript. I end nothing of #1431's (7243/8243) or #1430's.

Mac mini: not used.

--- ~00:43  cat > S/app/tree.js <<'EOF'
// ABOUTME: Throwaway: stand-in agent process tree; each level logs start/end and lives N seconds.
const { spawn } = require('child_process');
const fs = require('fs');
const [, , level, seconds, log, tag] = process.argv;
const lv = Number(level);
const line = (s) => fs.appendFileSync(log, `${new Date().toISOString()} ${s} level=${lv} pid=${process.pid} ppid=${process.ppid} ${tag}\n`);
line('start');
if (lv < 2) spawn(process.execPath, [__filename, String(lv + 1), seconds, log, tag], { stdio: 'ignore' });
process.on('SIGTERM', () => { line('sigterm'); process.exit(143); });
setTimeout(() => { line('end'); process.exit(0); }, Number(seconds) * 1000);
EOF
    sha256 of this body: 90b73eea2381fd71d7d11b9aee179ba2272c3606289258afa9212159937a4a1f

--- ~00:43  cat > S/app/activities.js <<'EOF'
// ABOUTME: Throwaway: activities for the #1429 spike — host pick, a dispatch that runs a heartbeating process tree, prepare, claude -p.
const { Context, ApplicationFailure } = require('@temporalio/activity');
const { spawn, spawnSync } = require('child_process');
const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const HOST = process.env.HOST_ID;
const WORKTREES = process.env.WORKTREES;
const log = (s) => console.log(`${new Date().toISOString()} [${HOST}] ${s}`);

async function pickHost({ excluded = [] } = {}) {
  if (excluded.includes(HOST)) {
    log(`pickHost: ${HOST} is excluded, failing so another worker takes it`);
    throw ApplicationFailure.retryable(`host ${HOST} excluded`, 'Excluded');
  }
  log(`pickHost -> ${HOST}`);
  return { host: HOST, machine: require('os').hostname() };
}

async function prepare({ card }) {
  log(`prepare card=${card}`);
  return `prepared ${card} on ${HOST}`;
}

async function dispatch({ card, seconds, retrySeconds }) {
  const info = Context.current().info;
  const secs = info.attempt > 1 && retrySeconds ? retrySeconds : seconds;
  const dir = path.join(WORKTREES, card); fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  const treeLog = path.join(dir, 'tree.log');
  const tag = `rigger-tree card=${card} attempt=${info.attempt} host=${HOST}`;
  const child = spawn(process.execPath, [path.join(__dirname, 'tree.js'), '1', String(secs), treeLog, tag], { cwd: dir, stdio: 'ignore' });
  log(`dispatch start card=${card} attempt=${info.attempt} seconds=${secs} treeRoot=${child.pid} workerPid=${process.pid}`);
  const beat = setInterval(() => Context.current().heartbeat({ attempt: info.attempt, root: child.pid }), 2000);
  try {
    const code = await new Promise((res, rej) => { child.on('exit', res); child.on('error', rej); });
    log(`dispatch end card=${card} attempt=${info.attempt} treeRoot=${child.pid} exit=${code}`);
    return { host: HOST, attempt: info.attempt, root: child.pid, exit: code };
  } finally { clearInterval(beat); }
}

async function claudeRun({ card, configDir, prompt }) {
  const info = Context.current().info;
  const env = { PATH: process.env.PATH, HOME: process.env.HOME, CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR: configDir };
  log(`claudeRun card=${card} attempt=${info.attempt} CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=${configDir}`);
  const r = spawnSync('claude', ['-p', prompt], { env, encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 120000 });
  const out = { host: HOST, exit: r.status, signal: r.signal, stdout: r.stdout, stderr: r.stderr, error: r.error && String(r.error) };
  log(`claudeRun result ${JSON.stringify(out)}`);
  return out;
}

module.exports = { pickHost, prepare, dispatch, claudeRun };
EOF
    sha256 of this body: c9dc05fca2cc8a38eb445ae4068b9e6132518a1748349aea4719d5458a96a7d9

// ABOUTME: Throwaway: card workflow v2 — v1 plus an unguarded prepare activity ahead of the dispatch.
const { proxyActivities } = require('@temporalio/workflow');
const { pinnedDispatch } = require('./wf-common');
async function card(input) {
  return pinnedDispatch(input, async (os) => {
    const { prepare } = proxyActivities({ taskQueue: `rigger-${os}`, startToCloseTimeout: '30s' });
    await prepare({ card: input.card });
  });
}
module.exports = { card };
EOF

--- ~00:43  cat > S/app/wf-v3.js <<'EOF'
// ABOUTME: Throwaway: card workflow v3 — the v2 prepare call guarded by patched().
const { proxyActivities, patched } = require('@temporalio/workflow');
const { pinnedDispatch } = require('./wf-common');
async function card(input) {
  return pinnedDispatch(input, async (os) => {
    if (patched('prepare-before-dispatch')) {
      const { prepare } = proxyActivities({ taskQueue: `rigger-${os}`, startToCloseTimeout: '30s' });
      await prepare({ card: input.card });
    }
  });
}
module.exports = { card };
EOF

--- ~00:43  cat > S/app/wf-common.js <<'EOF'
// ABOUTME: Throwaway: shared two-stage routing for the card workflow (OS queue pick, host-queue dispatch).
const { proxyActivities, sleep, log, ActivityFailure, TimeoutFailure, TimeoutType } = require('@temporalio/workflow');
const DISPATCH = { startToCloseTimeout: '20m', heartbeatTimeout: '10s', scheduleToStartTimeout: '30s',
  retry: { initialInterval: '1s', backoffCoefficient: 2, maximumInterval: '10s', maximumAttempts: 5 } };
async function pinnedDispatch(input, beforeDispatch) {
  const os = input.os; const excluded = [];
  const { pickHost } = proxyActivities({ taskQueue: `rigger-${os}`, startToCloseTimeout: '30s',
    retry: { initialInterval: '1s', backoffCoefficient: 1, maximumAttempts: 30 } });
  for (let round = 1; round <= 3; round++) {
    const picked = await pickHost({ excluded });
    log.info(`round ${round} picked ${picked.host}`);
    if (input.pauseAfterPick) await sleep(input.pauseAfterPick);
    if (beforeDispatch && round === 1) await beforeDispatch(os);
    const acts = proxyActivities({ ...DISPATCH, taskQueue: `host-${picked.host}` });
    try {
      if (input.claude) return await acts.claudeRun({ card: input.card, configDir: input.claude.configDir, prompt: input.claude.prompt });
      return await acts.dispatch({ card: input.card, seconds: input.seconds, retrySeconds: input.retrySeconds });
    } catch (e) {
      const c = e instanceof ActivityFailure ? e.cause : null;
      if (c instanceof TimeoutFailure && c.timeoutType === TimeoutType.SCHEDULE_TO_START) {
        log.info(`schedule-to-start timeout on ${picked.host}; re-picking`); excluded.push(picked.host); continue;
      }
      throw e;
    }
  }
  throw new Error('no host');
}
module.exports = { pinnedDispatch, DISPATCH };
EOF
    sha256 of this body: 817396162c7bb8937adfe0f5d0a797b03aea41c0137e16028834cd04f1f3de3c

--- ~00:43  cat > S/app/worker.js <<'EOF'
// ABOUTME: Throwaway: runs one host's workers — the OS queue (workflows, pickHost) and the host's own queue (dispatch).
const { Worker, NativeConnection } = require('@temporalio/worker');
const path = require('path');
const activities = require('./activities');
const HOST = process.env.HOST_ID, OS = process.env.OS_NAME, WF = process.env.WF_FILE || 'wf-v1.js';
(async () => {
  const connection = await NativeConnection.connect({ address: process.env.TEMPORAL_ADDRESS || 'localhost:7233' });
  const identity = `${HOST}/${require('os').hostname()}/${process.pid}`;
  console.log(`${new Date().toISOString()} worker starting identity=${identity} pid=${process.pid} os=${OS} wf=${WF} node=${process.version}`);
  const common = { connection, namespace: 'default', identity, activities, workflowsPath: path.join(__dirname, WF) };
  const w1 = await Worker.create({ ...common, taskQueue: `rigger-${OS}` });
  const w2 = await Worker.create({ ...common, taskQueue: `host-${HOST}` });
  console.log(`${new Date().toISOString()} worker polling rigger-${OS} and host-${HOST}`);
  await Promise.all([w1.run(), w2.run()]);
})().catch((e) => { console.error(`${new Date().toISOString()} worker error`, e); process.exit(1); });
EOF

#!/bin/bash
# ABOUTME: Throwaway: starts one laptop worker identity in the background and records its pid in the transcript.
S=$HOME/GitHub/rigger-worktrees/1429-spike-engineer/spikes/1429-spike-engineer
H=$1; WF=${2:-wf-v1.js}
mkdir -p $S/worktrees/$H $S/logs
cd $S/app
HOST_ID=$H OS_NAME=macos WF_FILE=$WF WORKTREES=$S/worktrees/$H TEMPORAL_ADDRESS=127.0.0.1:7233 nohup node worker.js >> $S/logs/worker-$H.log 2>&1 &
P=$!
echo $P > $S/logs/worker-$H.pid
printf '\n=== %s | host=laptop | cmd: start-mac-worker.sh %s %s -> worker pid=%s\n' "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%3NZ 2>/dev/null || date -u +%FT%TZ)" "$H" "$WF" "$P" >> $S/transcript.log
echo "mac worker $H pid=$P"
EOF

#!/bin/bash
# ABOUTME: Throwaway: starts a WSL or Windows worker through a background laptop SSH session and records that session's pid.
S=$HOME/GitHub/rigger-worktrees/1429-spike-engineer/spikes/1429-spike-engineer
KIND=$1; H=$2; WF=${3:-wf-v1.js}
mkdir -p $S/logs
if [ "$KIND" = wsl ]; then
  nohup ssh -o ServerAliveInterval=30 hostpc wsl.exe -e bash -s -- $H $WF < $S/remote/wsl-worker.sh >> $S/logs/session-$H.log 2>&1 &
else
  nohup ssh -o ServerAliveInterval=30 hostpc "C:\\Users\\user\\1429-spike-engineer\\win-worker.cmd $H $WF" < /dev/null >> $S/logs/session-$H.log 2>&1 &
fi
P=$!
printf '\n=== %s | host=laptop | cmd: start-remote-worker.sh %s %s %s -> laptop ssh session pid=%s\n' "$(date -u +%FT%TZ)" "$KIND" "$H" "$WF" "$P" >> $S/transcript.log
echo "$KIND worker $H via laptop ssh pid=$P"
EOF

#!/bin/bash
# ABOUTME: Throwaway: process listing of workers and stand-in trees on one host (laptop, wsl or windows), logged.
S=$HOME/GitHub/rigger-worktrees/1429-spike-engineer/spikes/1429-spike-engineer
case $1 in
  laptop) $S/t.sh laptop 'date -u +%FT%TZ; ps -axo pid,ppid,pgid,lstart,command | grep -E "rigger-tree|worker.js|PID" | grep -v grep' ;;
  wsl) $S/w.sh 'date -u +%FT%TZ; ps -eo pid,ppid,pgid,lstart,args | grep -E "rigger-tree|worker.js|PID" | grep -v grep' ;;
  windows) $S/p.sh '(Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"); Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter "Name=''node.exe''" | ForEach-Object { "{0,6} {1,6} {2} {3}" -f $_.ProcessId, $_.ParentProcessId, $_.CreationDate.ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"), $_.CommandLine }' ;;
esac
EOF

--- ~00:47:30  cat > S/killtest.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/bash
# ABOUTME: Throwaway: kill-mid-dispatch run for one OS — start a card, kill its worker -9, restart it, sample processes, export history.
S=$HOME/GitHub/rigger-worktrees/1429-spike-engineer/spikes/1429-spike-engineer
OS=$1; KIND=$2   # KIND: laptop|wsl|windows
WF=card-kill-$OS
TC="$S/tcli/temporal --address 127.0.0.1:7233"
now() { perl -MTime::HiRes=time -MPOSIX=strftime -e '$t=time; printf "%s.%03dZ", strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S",gmtime($t)), ($t-int($t))*1000'; }
pend() { $TC workflow describe -w $WF --output json 2>/dev/null | jq -c '[.pendingActivities[]? | {type:.activityType.name, state, attempt, lastWorkerIdentity, lastStartedTime, scheduledTime}]'; }
$S/t.sh laptop "$TC workflow start --type card --task-queue rigger-$OS --workflow-id $WF --input '{\"card\":\"$WF\",\"os\":\"$OS\",\"seconds\":300,\"retrySeconds\":20}'"
for i in $(seq 1 60); do P=$(pend); echo "$P" | grep -q '"type":"dispatch","state":"PENDING_ACTIVITY_STATE_STARTED"' && break; sleep 1; done
echo "pending: $P" | tee -a $S/transcript.log
ID=$(echo "$P" | jq -r '.[0].lastWorkerIdentity'); H=${ID%%/*}; WPID=${ID##*/}
echo "dispatch on identity=$ID host=$H workerpid=$WPID" | tee -a $S/transcript.log
sleep 6
echo "### listing before kill" | tee -a $S/transcript.log; $S/lst.sh $KIND
case $KIND in
  laptop) $S/t.sh laptop "perl -MTime::HiRes=time -MPOSIX=strftime -e '\$t=time; printf qq(kill-at %s.%03dZ\n), strftime(q(%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S),gmtime(\$t)), (\$t-int(\$t))*1000'; kill -9 $WPID; echo kill-rc=\$?" ;;
  wsl) $S/w.sh "echo kill-at \$(date -u +%FT%T.%3NZ); kill -9 $WPID; echo kill-rc=\$?" ;;
  windows) $S/p.sh "'kill-at ' + (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString('o'); Stop-Process -Id $WPID -Force; 'stop-rc=' + \$?" ;;
esac
echo "### listing just after kill" | tee -a $S/transcript.log; $S/lst.sh $KIND
echo "### restarting worker $H at $(now)" | tee -a $S/transcript.log
case $KIND in
  laptop) $S/start-mac-worker.sh $H ;;
  wsl) $S/start-remote-worker.sh wsl $H ;;
  windows) $S/start-remote-worker.sh win $H ;;
esac
for i in $(seq 1 90); do P=$(pend); echo "$(now) $P" >> $S/logs/poll-$WF.log; echo "$P" | grep -q '"attempt":2,"lastWorkerIdentity"' && echo "$P" | grep -q 'STARTED' && break; [ "$P" = "[]" ] && break; sleep 1; done
echo "$(now) after-restart pending: $P" | tee -a $S/transcript.log
sleep 3
echo "### listing with retry attempt running" | tee -a $S/transcript.log; $S/lst.sh $KIND
EOF
    sha256 of this body: c211a40dab9e481bbce0998b815bd0edcbb5c862be77aaa3850c8c4b92fff055

--- ~00:49:40  cat > S/pintest.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/bash
# ABOUTME: Throwaway: macOS pinning runs — stop the picked host's worker before dispatch (stop) or kill it mid-dispatch without restart (kill).
S=$HOME/GitHub/rigger-worktrees/1429-spike-engineer/spikes/1429-spike-engineer
MODE=$1; WF=pin-$MODE-macos
TC="$S/tcli/temporal --address 127.0.0.1:7233"
stamp() { perl -MTime::HiRes=time -MPOSIX=strftime -e '$t=time; printf "%s.%03dZ", strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S",gmtime($t)), ($t-int($t))*1000'; }
picked() { $TC workflow show -w $WF --output json | jq -r '[.events[] | select(.eventType=="EVENT_TYPE_ACTIVITY_TASK_COMPLETED") | .activityTaskCompletedEventAttributes.result.payloads[0].data | @base64d | fromjson | .host?] | first // empty'; }
pend() { $TC workflow describe -w $WF --output json 2>/dev/null | jq -c '[.pendingActivities[]? | {type:.activityType.name, state, attempt, lastWorkerIdentity, lastStartedTime, scheduledTime}]'; }
if [ $MODE = stop ]; then IN='{"card":"'$WF'","os":"macos","seconds":20,"pauseAfterPick":"20s"}'; else IN='{"card":"'$WF'","os":"macos","seconds":300,"retrySeconds":20}'; fi
$S/t.sh laptop "$TC workflow start --type card --task-queue rigger-macos --workflow-id $WF --input '$IN'"
for i in $(seq 1 30); do H=$(picked); [ -n "$H" ] && break; sleep 1; done
WPID=$(cat $S/logs/worker-$H.pid)
echo "$(stamp) picked host=$H worker pid=$WPID" | tee -a $S/transcript.log
if [ $MODE = kill ]; then
  for i in $(seq 1 30); do P=$(pend); echo "$P" | grep -q STARTED && break; sleep 1; done
  echo "pending: $P" | tee -a $S/transcript.log; sleep 5
  $S/lst.sh laptop
  $S/t.sh laptop "perl -MTime::HiRes=time -MPOSIX=strftime -e '\$t=time; printf qq(kill-at %s.%03dZ\n), strftime(q(%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S),gmtime(\$t)), (\$t-int(\$t))*1000'; kill -9 $WPID; echo kill-rc=\$?"
else
  $S/t.sh laptop "perl -MTime::HiRes=time -MPOSIX=strftime -e '\$t=time; printf qq(stop-at %s.%03dZ\n), strftime(q(%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S),gmtime(\$t)), (\$t-int(\$t))*1000'; kill -TERM $WPID; echo term-rc=\$?"
fi
for i in $(seq 1 150); do P=$(pend); echo "$(stamp) $P" >> $S/logs/poll-$WF.log; S2=$($TC workflow describe -w $WF --output json | jq -r .workflowExecutionInfo.status); [ "$S2" != WORKFLOW_EXECUTION_STATUS_RUNNING ] && break; echo "$P" | grep -v "$H/" | grep -q '"type":"dispatch","state":"PENDING_ACTIVITY_STATE_STARTED"' && break; sleep 1; done
echo "$(stamp) end-of-watch status=$S2 pending=$P" | tee -a $S/transcript.log
$S/lst.sh laptop
EOF
    sha256 of this body: 04286c599e2fd56751fb196d2e05be7bba8551f09a6f8082c8ca67c677fca317

--- ~01:41  cat > SP/fixpaths.pl <<'EOF'   (scratch helper that rewrote unresolvable backticked spans in the report)
# ABOUTME: Throwaway: rewrites backticked spans in the #1429 report that the path check cannot resolve.
use strict;
my $B = 'docs/spikes/temporal-dispatch-under-failure';
open my $m, '<', $ARGV[0] or die; my %miss = map { chomp; ($_ => 1) } <$m>; close $m;
my $file = $ARGV[1]; local $/; open my $f, '<', $file or die; my $t = <$f>; close $f;
my $infence = 0; my @out;
for my $line (split /\n/, $t, -1) {
  if ($line =~ /^```/) { $infence = !$infence; push @out, $line; next; }
  if (!$infence) {
    $line =~ s{`([^`]+)`}{
      my $s = $1; my $r;
      if (!$miss{$s}) { $r = "`$s`" }
      elsif ($s =~ m{^(logs/|histories/)} || $s =~ m{^(transcript\.txt|versions\.txt)$}) { $r = "`$B/$s`" }
      elsif ($s eq 'temporal-dispatch-under-failure/') { $r = "`$B/`" }
      elsif ($s =~ m{^(card-kill|pin-|ver-|claude-macos|smoke-).*\.json$}) { $r = "`$B/histories/$s`" }
      elsif ($s eq 'claude-projects-after.txt') { $r = "`$B/logs/laptop/$s`" }
      else { $r = $s }
      $r }ge;
  }
  push @out, $line;
}
open my $o, '>', $file or die; print $o join("\n", @out); close $o;
EOF
    sha256 of this body: 67bc2da6414024f7bb4c6f8a697cd01105e18705f057ee7803ff74ce2f25bbc9
